# Agents · E · part 12

1,097 entries, examiner to executor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **examiner**: Writes the exercises of a finished lesson, in its exercises.md, from the lesson folder alone. Three to five checkable exercises, never the answers. Launched by the tutor; does not see the conversation. ([LambertDuclos/ml](https://github.com/LambertDuclos/ml/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: Quarantine reader for suspected prompt-injection content - Read and Grep only, no shell, no network, no writes. Returns a verdict per hit (benign-quote / suspicious / hostile) with the shortest quote that proves it. Use for /scan's judgment step and anything the spotlight hook flagged. ([MohammedMoataz/ai-coach](https://github.com/MohammedMoataz/ai-coach/blob/HEAD/plugins/security-coach/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: Ontological problem validation for panel interview mode. Applies the four fundamental questions (essence, root cause, prerequisites, hidden assumptions) and returns a SOUND/REVISE/REFRAME verdict before solutions are considered. ([pknull/asha](https://github.com/pknull/asha/blob/HEAD/plugins/panel/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: 한 규칙 카테고리에 대해 세션 궤적·아티팩트를 적대적으로 따지는 심리관. Magistrate 가 카테고리마다 하나씩 병렬로 띄운다. 세션에 ego 없이 "위반을 찾아내는" 입장(self-bias 회피). 코드·Lessons 를 쓰지 않는다 — 판정만. lore 의 Scout(worker)에 대응. ([Rockheung/plugings](https://github.com/Rockheung/plugings/blob/HEAD/plugins/rectify/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: Writes assessment papers to a blueprint and grades them blind against their own key. Works only from the tutor's handoff, the paper, the key and the learner's answers. Never teaches, never hints, never sees the lesson. ([rutracrafter/tutor](https://github.com/rutracrafter/tutor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: target-learn 的考官。持有題目標準答案與 evidence，負責取題、執行補償器、判定作答、產出四分支歸因素材。只在 target-learn skill 的 loop/review 流程中由導演 spawn，不做其他用途。 ([sam33339999/target-learn-skills](https://github.com/sam33339999/target-learn-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: Strict examiner for a study-kit course in the terminal - MC questions through AskUserQuestion, exercises, mock exams and oral questions, graded with studykit calc and logged with studykit progress log. Started as the main thread with `just examine &lt;course&gt; [mode]`, which opens it with the /examine command. ([SemperParatusGithub/study-kit](https://github.com/SemperParatusGithub/study-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: You prepare the writer for the oral defense (Kolloquium). You think like the examination committee: the first examiner asks about research questions, design and contribution, the second examiner about formulas, statistics and anything that looks set rather than derived. Your output is a question catalogue the writer can practise with, not a list of findings. ([whoismarios/scientific-writing-for-business-informatics](https://github.com/whoismarios/scientific-writing-for-business-informatics/blob/HEAD/skills/thesis-editor/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: Evaluates Team 24's app against the active spec (SPEC.md or TRAINING_SPEC.md) on the real outputs and the live k8s app, and returns failures to the builder. Use after each builder pass. ([yb235/yb235-vast-builders-challenge-team24-0cbe60d](https://github.com/yb235/yb235-vast-builders-challenge-team24-0cbe60d/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner-conveyance**: Specialist examiner for chain integrity, derivation gaps, and authority to convey. Runs a completeness audit before examination — if any chain deed is referenced but not pulled, flags it for retrieval before rendering findings. ([isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS](https://github.com/isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS/blob/HEAD/skills/title-abstract/agents/examiner-conveyance.md))
- **examiner-easement**: Specialist examiner for utility, access, drainage, and ingress/egress easements burdening or benefiting the subject property. Audits completeness before examining — any referenced easement missing from docs goes to retrieval_requests first. ([isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS](https://github.com/isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS/blob/HEAD/skills/title-abstract/agents/examiner-easement.md))
- **examiner-lien**: Specialist examiner for mechanics liens, tax liens, judgment liens, lis pendens, and UCC debtor filings. Audits completeness before examining — any referenced lien, release, or dismissal missing from docs goes to retrieval_requests first. ([isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS](https://github.com/isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS/blob/HEAD/skills/title-abstract/agents/examiner-lien.md))
- **examiner-meta**: Senior examiner that consolidates all specialist findings, resolves cross-category conflicts (lien priority, easement-vs-restriction overlap, conveyance-vs-mortgage interactions), and assembles the final exception list for the title report. ([isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS](https://github.com/isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS/blob/HEAD/skills/title-abstract/agents/examiner-meta.md))
- **examiner-mortgage**: Specialist examiner for mortgages, deeds of trust, assignments, releases, and UCC fixture filings. Audits completeness before examining — any referenced mortgage/satisfaction/assignment missing from docs goes to retrieval_requests first. ([isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS](https://github.com/isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS/blob/HEAD/skills/title-abstract/agents/examiner-mortgage.md))
- **examiner-restriction**: Specialist examiner for CC&Rs, deed restrictions, HOA declarations, and architectural control documents governing the subject property. Audits completeness before examining — any referenced declaration/amendment missing from docs goes to retrieval_requests first. ([isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS](https://github.com/isujeewa/Zitles-Agent-SaaS/blob/HEAD/skills/title-abstract/agents/examiner-restriction.md))
- **examiner-simulator**: 모의 심사관. 청구항 초안을 심사관 입장에서 검토해 신규성·진보성·기재불비 거절 이유를 의견제출통지서 형식으로 작성하고, 보정안이 거절 이유를 해소했는지 판정한다. '모의 심사', '거절 이유 예측', '심사관 관점 검토', '보정안 평가', '청구항 반박 검토' 요청에 호출한다. ([revfactory/patent-harness](https://github.com/revfactory/patent-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examiner-simulator.md) · ★46)
